HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001252/B001","root_001252/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0c537b043d68078ad75d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:4:2\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:4:3\",\"root_001252\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kalem, kesilip yontularak hazırlanmış maddi bir araç olarak seçme, ayırma ve biçim verme yoluyla öğretir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001252/B001","root_001252/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_152de44245162c52bef4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:4:3\",\"root_001252\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yontulmuş kalem ucu ile sonradan yakalanan perçem arasında, bilgi için biçim verilen uç ile sahte iradenin zorla denetlenen önü arasında uzak bir bedensel benzerlik kurulabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1adfd1ba156a9b6fdc18","text":"2. **Knowing externalized as a distinguishing mark.** `ع ل م:B002` supplies a visible mark that distinguishes and points, and `ق ل م:B003` supplies the writing instrument. Later in the window, `رآه` and `أرأيت` (96:7, 96:9) foreground the limits of immediate seeing. The mark branch’s role is to let knowledge persist and direct attention beyond the present visual field. I infer that written marks extend perception and make distinctions inspectable. `علّم بالقلم` thus reads as teaching through durable signs, not only through an object called a pen.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_001252/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4f52aceaf72aa7294ae8","text":"1. **The pen as a tool that forms by selective cutting.** `ق ل م:B003` supplies the trimmed writing reed, while `ق ل م:B001` supplies trimming or paring a hard thing bit by bit. The pen follows measured creation (`خ ل ق:B001`) and precedes teaching what was unknown (96:5). The cutting branch gives the instrument an active formative role: it segments, shapes, and regularizes material into marks. The packet supplies the material analogy; I infer that pen-mediated teaching works through selection and articulation. The ayah therefore presents instruction as disciplined formation, not mere storage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8386f94658548cadc688","text":"The later sight sequence in 96:7 and 96:9 made `ع ل م:B002` more than a graphic analogy: pen-made marks become a corrective to self-enclosed seeing. Meanwhile, 96:6–9 showed that taught intelligibility does not compel sound self-judgment. That later failure sharpened, rather than canceled, the reading of the pen as a disciplined instrument of distinction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002","root_001252/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_89a509289b0052d4fe24","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:4:2\",\"root_001040\",[\"B002\"]],[\"96:4:3\",\"root_001252\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kalemle öğretme, bilgiyi anlık görüşün dışına taşıyan, kalıcı ve ayırt edilebilir işaretler üretir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_96cf9d71103908af2add","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:4:2\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Öğretim anlaşılabilirlik ve yargılama imkânı verir, fakat insanı doğru öz değerlendirmeye zorlamaz; sonraki yanlış görme bu imkânın kötü kullanılabildiğini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001252/B004","root_001252/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9e2335f3faf5fd0d1c08","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:4:3\",\"root_001252\",[\"B004\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kalemin kura çubuğu ve kesilip ayrılmış arazi çağrışımları, öğretimi yazı aktarmanın yanında payları ve sınırları görünür kılan bir işlem gibi düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c385ae754c823ad8178c","text":"3. **Disclosure that enables judgment but does not compel it.** `ع ل م:B001` covers making a thing clear, learning, and teaching. The sequence moves from divine teaching (96:4–5) to a human misjudging himself as sufficient and then restraining another (96:6–9). The branch supplies disclosure; the later conduct shows that disclosure can be received without governing self-assessment. I infer a distinction between epistemic capacity and ethical use. The ayah reads as the provision of intelligibility whose misuse cannot be blamed on an absence of teaching.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001040/B002","root_001252/B001","root_001252/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d5c1e0446c5c9e3c562a","text":"1. Teaching by the pen is teaching by a trimmed instrument that converts cutting into inscription. Evidence: `ع ل م B001` knowing/teaching; `ق ل م B001` trimming/paring a hard thing; `ق ل م B003` trimmed writing reed/pen. Structural cue: the instrument follows the verb `عَلَّمَ` with `بـ`, as `باسم` followed `ٱقْرَأْ` in 96:1. Mechanism: a pared reed or hard implement becomes a means of transmitting marks. Abductive move: the packet gives the pen's material origin in cutting; I infer that disciplined cutting makes durable teaching possible. Reading change: the pen is not an abstract symbol of literacy; it is a prepared tool whose material discipline channels knowledge. 2. The pen also activates marking, boundary, and allocation. Evidence: `ع ل م B002` distinguishing mark/landmark, `ق ل م B004` casting stick for lots, `ق ل م B008` cut-off region of land. Structural cue: the passage moves from created form to taught mark, then later to moral/legal discrimination between guidance, denial, and error. Mechanism: inscription and lots both divide and make things legible: who is on guidance, who forbids, who lies. Abductive move: the packet supplies mark/cut/lot branches; I infer that “pen” participates in a larger mechanism of differentiation. Reading change: teaching by the pen reads as producing discernible marks and boundaries, not only written content. 3. The pen's trimming activates restraint against later transgression. Evidence: `ق ل م B001` paring bit by bit, `ط غ ي B001/B002` overstepping/overflowing, `ن ه ي B001` restraint. Structural cue: trimmed instrument appears before the human overflows in 96:6. Mechanism: teaching requires a cut-down, shaped medium; arrogance is an untrimmed excess. Abductive move: the packet supplies material trimming and later boundary-exceeding; I infer a contrast between form-making and overflow. Reading change: the ayah becomes an image of disciplined form before disorder.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ec2fde801ead75567ad5","text":"The later `ناصية` focus made the pen unexpectedly bodily: both pen and forelock are front/tip points that can be shaped, marked, or seized.
